A: "Astrosat is India's first dedicated multi-wavelength space observatory. It was launched on a PSLV-XL rocket on 28 September 2015. Stauntonia belongs to the Lardizabalaceae family and is named after George Staunton, who introduced it to Britain from China in the 19th century. Q: What Old English poem commemorates the capturing of the five main towns of Danish Mercia?
A: "The Old English poem that commemorates the capturing of the five main towns of Danish Mercia is the \"Capture of the Five Boroughs\" (also known as \"Redemption of the Five Boroughs\"). This poem is an Old English chronicle poem that marks the recapture by King Edmund I of the Five Boroughs in 942. The Five Boroughs, or the Five Boroughs of the Danelaw, were the key towns of Danish Mercia: Derby, Leicester, Lincoln, Nottingham, and Stamford."
